</div></div></div><div class="blog-post-content__section--text"><p><strong>The above analysis revealed ties between accounts ExpertData, ShinyHunters, and whysodankk</strong> (aka whysodank, fs0c131y). The links between ShinyHunters and other groups are out of the scope of this article.</p>
<p>Going back to our primary task to understand the quality of databases and the level of threat actor for the post of ExpertData on October 28, 2020, we can make the following conclusions:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>The databases are most probably authentic.</strong> No other posts with the same databases published earlier by other users were revealed. During the analysis links between ExpertData and ShinyHunters were uncovered. Several databases published by ExpertData were most probably exfiltrated by ShinyHunters and subsequently transferred to ExpertData. Publishing of unique data leaks is typical for ShinyHunters group members.</li>
<li><strong>It is supposed that ShinyHunters was behind these leaks.</strong> ShinyHunters is a famous hacker group with a strong background and skills. It is assumed that the group consists of several people, therefore they can publish new leaks using different accounts. If our conclusion is true, then the databases were obtained as a result of the affected companies&#8217; compromise. In such situations it is highly recommended to start incident response and investigation as soon as possible.</li>
</ol>
<p>After the link between ExpertData and ShinyHunters was uncovered, we decided to further analyze ShinyHunters&#8217; relation to other members of the forum and find other accounts that can potentially be linked to this threat actor. However, to find other links to ShinyHunters, we would have had to analyze around a hundred messages of ShinyHunters and a bunch of posts belonging to their potential members. To simplify the research, we decided to use ML-algorithms based on semantic text analysis.</p>
<h2>Semantic analysis</h2>
<p>The first part of the article represented the manual analysis of the attackers&#8217; profiles and posts on the underground forum. Group-IB, however, is trying to automate its methodologies of cyber investigations and resorts to ML-algorithms to analyze dark web and build correlations between various posts in the underground.</p>
<p>We, therefore, decided to check the same range of data utilizing Natural Language Processing (hereinafter &#8211; NLP) algorithms to reveal multiaccounts of the same person or various accounts of the same group of people. The analysis was carried out based on the assumption that the semantics of messages from the same person or sometimes even members of the same group should be similar.</p>
<p>The sequence of data collection and processing is shown below:</p>
<ul>
<li>The collection of all posts from the RaidForums (topic, message, nickname, date and time);</li>
<li>The filtering of messages with a minimum length of less than 100 characters to exclude semantically insignificant texts;</li>
<li>The vector representation of texts obtained using the BERT neural network model (12-layer, 768-hidden, 12-heads, 110M parameters).</li>
</ul>
<p>For further analysis, all message vectors (N) belonging to user <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> were selected. For each such vector, the top 10 nearest vectors of other users&#8217; messages were found (by the Euclidean distance). As a result, we obtained a matrix of dimension N*10 (example of the matrix is in the table below), with the nicknames of published messages as well as its elements. Then we filtered out repeated messages and messages whose Euclidean distance exceeds the specified threshold. The threshold was calculated empirically by comparing the proximity of different messages.</p>
<p>As a result, we received a list of messages semantically close to the messages of <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>. All the messages were grouped by the users who published them (<em><strong>matches</strong></em>). Users who have only one semantically close message (1 match) were excluded from the subsequent analysis to decrease false correlations. The last step was averaging the Euclidean distance across all messages for each of the users. The resulting values will be the <em><strong>scores</strong></em> we were looking for.</p>
<p>It is important to note that this metric is not symmetric: if, for example, a User A account analysis determined their link to User B, such a link might not be found as a result of the analysis of User B. This is due to the fact that we have limited the search to the top 10 nearest vectors for each user message. Surely, such sets of the nearest vectors, even for semantically close messages, may differ. For this reason, the similar analysis for the found users may not display the link with <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>.</p>
<p><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/27-min.jpg.webp" type="image/webp"><img decoding="async" class="alignnone size-full wp-image-25443 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201680%20900'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="" width="1680" height="900" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/27-min.jpg"><noscript><img decoding="async" class="alignnone size-full wp-image-25443 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/27-min.jpg" alt="" width="1680" height="900"></noscript></picture></p>
<p>Here is an example of calculation for User_3, based on the results of the first 3 messages in the table above:</p>
<pre>S = (3.3 + 1.1 + 2.2) / 3 = 2.2
Matches (semantically close messages): 3
Average distance (score): 2.2</pre>
<p>The results of the analysis of the ShinyHunters messages are presented in fig. 27 (the total number of user posts at the time of writing is indicated in square brackets).</p>
<div id="attachment_25444-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/28-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25444-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25444 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20688'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of ShinyHunters posts" width="1600" height="688" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/28-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25444-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25444 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/28-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of ShinyHunters posts" width="1600" height="688"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25444-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 27 &#8211; The result of the analysis of ShinyHunters posts</p></div>
<p>The analysis showed that accounts <strong>fs0c131y, MyBiggyBruteBolt, J4ckd0x, Megadimarus, johnlockejrr, Omnipotent, Troy Hunt, Databases</strong> and <strong>FluffyBunnyFufu</strong> published several messages that are semantically close to <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>&#8216; posts.</p>
<p>In fig.27, we see that the number of semantically close messages (matches) between ShinyHunters and other users is small, so it is incorrect to unequivocally state that there is a connection between them. However, the values of this metric give us a reason to take a closer look at possible relationships.</p>
<p><strong>It is noteworthy that the semantic analysis doesn&#8217;t reveal the strong link between ExpertData and ShinyHunters accounts.</strong> Below is the metric for the ExpertData account.</p>
<div id="attachment_25445-2" style="width: 794px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/29-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25445-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25445 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20784%20344'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of ExpertData posts" width="784" height="344" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/29-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25445-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25445 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/29-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of ExpertData posts" width="784" height="344"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25445-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 28 &#8211; The result of the analysis of ExpertData posts</p></div>
<p>The distance to <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> messages is too high, which can be explained by three assumptions:</p>
<ol>
<li><strong>ExpertData</strong> is a data broker who worked for this group. Big groups usually change data brokers quite often and don&#8217;t interact with them closely. That is why the format of the messages of a data broker could be different from ShinyHunters posts.</li>
<li><strong>ExpertData</strong> has few posts;</li>
<li>Semantic analysis may not work for dialogues in which each message carries its own semantic meaning. Let&#8217;s say that threat actors might discuss the same topic, but each of the messages in this thread will have their unique idea, and semantic analysis will not be able to reveal links between these separate messages.</li>
</ol>
<p>As you can see from fig.27, there is a link between <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> and <strong>fs0c131y</strong> (aka whysodankk) accounts. The link was earlier shown in the part of manual analysis and confirmed by semantic analysis.</p>
<div id="attachment_25446-2" style="width: 1264px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/30-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25446-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25446 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201254%20512'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="he result of the semantic analysis of fs0c131y and whysodankk posts" width="1254" height="512" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/30-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25446-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25446 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/30-min-1.png" alt="he result of the semantic analysis of fs0c131y and whysodankk posts" width="1254" height="512"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25446-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 29 &#8211; The result of the semantic analysis of fs0c131y and whysodankk posts</p></div>
<p>As you can see different nicknames of the same account are identified by the algorithm.</p>
<p>The link of <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> with users <strong>MyBiggyBruteBolt</strong> and <strong>johnlockejrr</strong> was found, because format of the messages is similar, however most of the posts contain the same note: &#8220;<em>This forum account currently has an ongoing scam report, please beware trading. Details: Please respond to this scam report.</em>&#8221;</p>
<div id="attachment_25447-2" style="width: 2134px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/31-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25447-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25447 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%202124%20394'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters/MyBiggyBruteBolt users" width="2124" height="394" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/31-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25447-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25447 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/31-min-1.png" alt="Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters/MyBiggyBruteBolt users" width="2124" height="394"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25447-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 30 &#8211; Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters/MyBiggyBruteBolt users</p></div>
<div id="attachment_25448-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/32-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25448-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25448 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20212'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters/johnlockejrr users" width="1600" height="212" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/32-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25448-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25448 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/32-min-1.png" alt="Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters/johnlockejrr users" width="1600" height="212"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25448-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 31 &#8211; Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters/johnlockejrr users</p></div>
<p>Metrics for <strong>MyBiggyBruteBolt</strong> and <strong>johnlockejrr</strong> are presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25449-2" style="width: 1378px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/33-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25449-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25449 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201368%20610'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of MyBiggyBruteBolt posts" width="1368" height="610" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/33-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25449-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25449 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/33-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of MyBiggyBruteBolt posts" width="1368" height="610"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25449-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 32 &#8211; The result of the analysis of MyBiggyBruteBolt posts</p></div>
<div id="attachment_25450-2" style="width: 994px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/34-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25450-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25450 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20984%20452'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of johnlockejrr posts" width="984" height="452" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/34-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25450-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25450 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/34-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of johnlockejrr posts" width="984" height="452"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25450-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 33 &#8211; The result of the analysis of johnlockejrr posts</p></div>
<p>In our opinion, these accounts are not relevant to <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> and could be considered as false positives.</p>
<p>According to fig.27, there is a link between <strong>J4ckd0x</strong> and <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> accounts. The account J4ckd0x was created back in 2016.</p>
<div id="attachment_25451-2" style="width: 1253px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/35-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25451-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25451 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201243%20673'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="J4ckd0x account on RaidForum" width="1243" height="673" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/35-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25451-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25451 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/35-min-1.png" alt="J4ckd0x account on RaidForum" width="1243" height="673"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25451-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 34 &#8211; J4ckd0x account on RaidForum</p></div>
<p>According to the content of J4ckd0x&#8217;s messages, his interests lie mostly in selling databases. The metric for this account is presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25452-2" style="width: 1161px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/36-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25452-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25452 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201151%20447'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of J4ckd0x's posts" width="1151" height="447" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/36-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25452-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25452 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/36-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of J4ckd0x's posts" width="1151" height="447"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25452-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 35 &#8211; The result of the analysis of J4ckd0x&#8217;s posts</p></div>
<p>On the screenshot below, you can see that the format of J4ckd0x&#8217;s posts offering databases for sale is similar to those of ShinyHunters.</p>
<div id="attachment_25453-2" style="width: 1515px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/37-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25453-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25453 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201505%20273'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Semantic proximity in posts of J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters" width="1505" height="273" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/37-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25453-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25453 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/37-min-1.png" alt="Semantic proximity in posts of J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters" width="1505" height="273"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25453-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 36 &#8211; Semantic proximity in posts of J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters</p></div>
<p><strong>There are two matches in messages of J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters that relate to two databases.</strong> <strong>J4ckd0x</strong> reposted (September 2020) one database that was first published by <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> (April 2020). Dunzo database was published by ShinyHunters (July 2020) before the post of J4ckd0x (September 2020). The format of the message posted by ShinyHunters about Sonicbids database is similar to the format of J4ckd0x&#8217;s post about another leakage. The format of the message posted by ShinyHunters about GGumim.com.kr database is similar to the format of the J4ckd0x&#8217;s post about the Dunzo leakage.</p>
<div id="attachment_25454-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/38-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25454-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25454 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20697'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Posts published by J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters" width="1600" height="697" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/38-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25454-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25454 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/38-min-1.png" alt="Posts published by J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters" width="1600" height="697"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25454-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 37 &#8211; Posts published by J4ckd0x and ShinyHunters and written using the same format</p></div>
<p>Based on the foregoing, <strong>we concluded that accounts ShinyHunters and J4ckd0x could belong to the same group or at least be partners.</strong></p>
<p>According to fig.27, there is a link between <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> and <strong>Megadimarus</strong> accounts. Megadimarus account was created on May 4, 2020 on the RaidForums and is banned at the time of writing.</p>
<div id="attachment_25455-2" style="width: 1099px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/39-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25455-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25455 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201089%20894'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Megadimarus profile on RaidForums" width="1089" height="894" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/39-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25455-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25455 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/39-min-1.png" alt="Megadimarus profile on RaidForums" width="1089" height="894"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25455-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 38 &#8211; Megadimarus profile on RaidForums</p></div>
<p>The metric for <strong>Megadimarius</strong> is presented below and is symmetric with <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>.</p>
<div id="attachment_25456-2" style="width: 1190px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/40-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25456-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25456 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201180%20210'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of Megadimarus posts" width="1180" height="210" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/40-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25456-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25456 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/40-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of Megadimarus posts" width="1180" height="210"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25456-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 39 &#8211; The result of the analysis of Megadimarus posts</p></div>
<p>Most of his messages were removed from the visible content of the forums however thanks to the capabilities of Group-IB&#8217;s proprietary products the removed contents are preserved and analyzed as well. Most of the Megadimarus posts are related to selling databases. Moreover, some of them are associated with the activities of ShinyHunters predecessors. In the report of cybersecurity researcher Vinny Troia the relationship between ShinyHunters and Megadimarus was also shown.</p>
<p>The metric shows a similarity of posts&#8217; format for <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> and <strong>Megadimarus</strong>. Moreover, in this particular case every match is related to the same database (<em><strong>Appen.com</strong></em> and <em><strong>HomeChef</strong></em> leakages).</p>
<div id="attachment_25457-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/41-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25457-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25457 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20588'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters and Megadimarus" width="1600" height="588" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/41-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25457-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25457 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/41-min-1.png" alt="Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters and Megadimarus" width="1600" height="588"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25457-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 40 &#8211; Semantically close posts of ShinyHunters and Megadimarus</p></div>
<p>Based on the analysis of <strong>Megadimarius</strong>&#8216;s posts and interests we concluded that there could be a link between this account and <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>. It could be members of the same group or they could be partners.</p>
<p>Another account for the analysis from fig.27 is <strong>Omnipotent</strong>. It is one of the administrator accounts created back in 2015.</p>
<div id="attachment_25458-2" style="width: 1011px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/42-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25458-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25458 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201001%20603'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Omnipotent profile on RaidForums" width="1001" height="603" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/42-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25458-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25458 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/42-min-1.png" alt="Omnipotent profile on RaidForums" width="1001" height="603"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25458-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 41 &#8211; Omnipotent profile on RaidForums</p></div>
<p>One of the matches between <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> and <strong>Omnipotent</strong> is due to the following post.</p>
<div id="attachment_25459-2" style="width: 1515px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/43-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25459-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25459 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201505%20615'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Post of Omnipotent about an uploaded database" width="1505" height="615" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/43-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25459-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25459 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/43-min-1.png" alt="Post of Omnipotent about an uploaded database" width="1505" height="615"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25459-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 42 &#8211; Post of Omnipotent about an uploaded database</p></div>
<p>It was reposted by <strong>Omnipotent</strong> from <strong>GnosticPlayers1</strong> account which is now banned. The format is similar to a format of <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> post about selling databases. <strong>GnosticPlayers1</strong> interest lies in selling databases and this nickname is similar to the name of the group Gnostic Players.</p>
<p>Two messages of Omnipotent have similar format with the format of ShinyHunter&#8217;s posts. Since Omnipotent account has thousands of messages, and only one (excluding the reposted one) having the format similar to ShinyHunters, as well as knowing that Omnipotent is the forum administrator, we consider this match as a false positive.</p>
<p>The metric for Omnipotent is presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25460-2" style="width: 972px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/44-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25460-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25460 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20962%20478'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of Omnipotent posts" width="962" height="478" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/44-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25460-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25460 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/44-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of Omnipotent posts" width="962" height="478"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25460-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 43 &#8211; The result of the analysis of Omnipotent posts</p></div>
<p>Another account from fig.27 is <strong>Troy Hunt</strong>. This account was created in June 2019 and impersonated the name and photo of the famous cybersecurity researcher Troy Hunt.</p>
<div id="attachment_25461-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/45-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25461-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25461 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20789'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Screenshot of Troy Hunt (aka Jnx3cx) account" width="1600" height="789" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/45-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25461-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25461 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/45-min-1.png" alt="Screenshot of Troy Hunt (aka Jnx3cx) account" width="1600" height="789"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25461-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 44 &#8211; Screenshot of Troy Hunt (aka Jnx3cx) account</p></div>
<p>The history of the account&#8217;s nickname change is presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25462-2" style="width: 811px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/46-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25462-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25462 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20801%20131'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Username history for Troy Hunt/Jnx3cx account" width="801" height="131" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/46-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25462-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25462 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/46-min-1.png" alt="Username history for Troy Hunt/Jnx3cx account" width="801" height="131"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25462-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 45 &#8211; Username history for Troy Hunt/Jnx3cx account</p></div>
<p>The main interest of the account is selling databases some of which are associated with the activities of <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> predecessors. The format of the messages is also correlated with the one of <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>.</p>
<div id="attachment_25463-2" style="width: 918px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/47-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25463-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25463 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20908%20316'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Posts published by Troy Hunt and ShinyHunters" width="908" height="316" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/47-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25463-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25463 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/47-min-1.png" alt="Posts published by Troy Hunt and ShinyHunters" width="908" height="316"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25463-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 46 &#8211; Posts published by Troy Hunt and ShinyHunters and written using the same format</p></div>
<p><strong>The algorithm shows the similarity of messages of ShinyHunters with the posts of Troy Hunt</strong> about selling Canva and Zynga (now deleted from the visible contents of RaidForums). Moreover, Troy Hunt posted a message on behalf of Gnostic Players (is supposed to be a predecessor of ShinyHunters according to the research of Vinny Troia).</p>
<div id="attachment_25464-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/48-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25464-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25464 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20876'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Replies to the deleted post of Troy Hunt with a reference to GnosticPlayers" width="1600" height="876" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/48-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25464-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25464 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/48-min-1.png" alt="Replies to the deleted post of Troy Hunt with a reference to GnosticPlayers" width="1600" height="876"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25464-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 47 &#8211; Replies to the deleted post of Troy Hunt with a reference to GnosticPlayers</p></div>
<div id="attachment_25465-2" style="width: 992px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/49-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25465-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25465 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20982%20280'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Post of Troy Hunt with a reference to GnosticPlayers and Zynga database" width="982" height="280" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/49-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25465-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25465 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/49-min-1.png" alt="Post of Troy Hunt with a reference to GnosticPlayers and Zynga database" width="982" height="280"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25465-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 48 &#8211; Post of Troy Hunt with a reference to GnosticPlayers and Zynga database</p></div>
<p>The metric for the <strong>Troy Hunt</strong> account is presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25466-2" style="width: 1354px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/50-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25466-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25466 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201344%20666'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of Troy Hunt posts" width="1344" height="666" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/50-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25466-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25466 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/50-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of Troy Hunt posts" width="1344" height="666"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25466-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 49 &#8211; The result of the analysis of Troy Hunt posts</p></div>
<div id="attachment_25467-2" style="width: 1400px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/51-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25467-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25467 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201390%20418'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of Jnx3cx (aka Troy Hunt) posts" width="1390" height="418" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/51-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25467-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25467 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/51-min-1.png" alt="The result of the analysis of Jnx3cx (aka Troy Hunt) posts" width="1390" height="418"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25467-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 50 &#8211; The result of the analysis of Jnx3cx (aka Troy Hunt) posts</p></div>
<p><strong>After analyzing the Troy Hunt account, his posts and interests, we concluded that its link with ShinyHunters could be relevant meaning the accounts could belong to the same group or at least be partners.</strong></p>
<p>Next account from fig.27 is <strong>Databases</strong> created back in 2015.</p>
<div id="attachment_25468-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/52-min-1.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25468-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25468 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20827'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Databases profile on RaidForums" width="1600" height="827" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/52-min-1.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25468-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25468 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/52-min-1.png" alt="Databases profile on RaidForums" width="1600" height="827"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25468-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 51 &#8211; Databases profile on RaidForums</p></div>
<p>The history of name changes is presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25469-2" style="width: 781px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/53-min.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25469-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25469 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%20771%20237'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Username history for Databases" width="771" height="237" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/53-min.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25469-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25469 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/53-min.png" alt="Username history for Databases" width="771" height="237"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25469-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 52 &#8211; Username history for Databases</p></div>
<p>The account started to post databases only in 2019. In August 2021, the format of their messages about selling databases started to resemble <strong>ShinyHunter</strong>&#8216;s posts. That is why <strong>we suppose that Databases probably became a partner of ShinyHunters in 2021.</strong> ShinyHunters themselves have used this format of messages since 2020.</p>
<p>The metric for Databases is presented below.</p>
<div id="attachment_25470-2" style="width: 1190px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/54-min.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25470-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25470 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201180%20528'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of Databases' posts" width="1180" height="528" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/54-min.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25470-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25470 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/54-min.png" alt="The result of the analysis of Databases' posts" width="1180" height="528"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25470-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 53 &#8211; The result of the analysis of Databases&#8217; posts</p></div>
<p><strong>As you can see from the metric Troy Hunt (aka Jnx3cx) and Databases are close to each other as well.</strong></p>
<p>The last account for the analysis is <strong>FluffyBunnyFufu</strong>, created back in 2018.</p>
<div id="attachment_25471-2" style="width: 1263px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/55-min.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25471-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25471 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201253%20769'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="FluffyBunnyFufu (aka Ceech, FunnyBunnyHere) profile on RaidForums" width="1253" height="769" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/55-min.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25471-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25471 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/55-min.png" alt="FluffyBunnyFufu (aka Ceech, FunnyBunnyHere) profile on RaidForums" width="1253" height="769"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25471-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 54 &#8211; FluffyBunnyFufu (aka Ceech, FunnyBunnyHere) profile on RaidForums</p></div>
<div id="attachment_25472-2" style="width: 1263px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/56-min.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25472-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25472 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201253%20209'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Username history for Ceech/FluffyBunnyFufu/FluffyBunnyHere account" width="1253" height="209" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/56-min.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25472-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25472 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/56-min.png" alt="Username history for Ceech/FluffyBunnyFufu/FluffyBunnyHere account" width="1253" height="209"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25472-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 55 &#8211; Username history for Ceech/FluffyBunnyFufu/FluffyBunnyHere account</p></div>
<p><strong>Almost all mutual threads of the users relate to databases.</strong> One of <strong>FluffyBunnyFufu</strong>&#8216;s posts with a leak publication is similar in format to one of <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>&#8216; posts. Additionally, FluffyBunnyFufu joined a dispute initiated against ShinyHunters with fs0c131y/whysodankk. FluffyBunnyFufu joined the talk to defend the position of ShinyHunters. This tactic is common for the members of ShinyHunters group as we noted during the Manual analysis part of this research.</p>
<div id="attachment_25473-2" style="width: 1568px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/57-min.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25473-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25473 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201558%20622'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="Posts published by FluffyBunnyFufu and ShinyHunters" width="1558" height="622" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/57-min.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25473-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25473 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/57-min.png" alt="Posts published by FluffyBunnyFufu and ShinyHunters" width="1558" height="622"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25473-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 56 &#8211; Posts published by FluffyBunnyFufu and ShinyHunters and written using the same format</p></div>
<p>According to the available data, it cannot be stated for sure if there is a clear connection between these two accounts, and it may be just a coincidence. However, it is clear that <strong>FluffyBunnyFufu</strong> keeps an eye on <strong>ShinyHunters</strong>&#8216;s activities.</p>
<p>We can conclude that accounts J4ckd0x, Megadimarus, Troy Hunt, and Databases either occasionally used the same message format to sell databases, or they are related to each other and ShinyHunters. However, the majority of ShinyHunters&#8217; posts about selling databases have been using the same format since 2020, so we tend to think that this format is typical for the group.</p>
<div id="attachment_25474-2" style="width: 1610px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/58-min.png.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25474-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25474 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201600%20897'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The result of the analysis of ShinyHunters' posts" width="1600" height="897" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/58-min.png"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25474-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25474 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/58-min.png" alt="The result of the analysis of ShinyHunters' posts" width="1600" height="897"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25474-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 57 &#8211; The result of the analysis of ShinyHunters&#8217; posts</p></div>
<p>Another argument is that the databases which were posted by these accounts are associated with <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> or their predecessors. According to Vinny Troia&#8217;s research, <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> is a successor to NSFW and Gnostic Players groups.</p>
<p><strong>The semantic analysis along with our manual research has also shown that the higher the distance between the messages of two different accounts is, the weaker their connection is.</strong> This sounds reasonable from a theoretical point of view and our analysis has confirmed it.</p>
<h2>Conclusions</h2>
<p>In this article, we wanted to show <strong>several methods of analysis using which researchers can establish possible links between various accounts in the underground</strong>. To showcase them, we used the example of infamous group <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> known for their unique and resonant leaks.</p>
<p>The starting point of our research was a message from ExpertData, a relatively new account with several unique leaks that appeared in September 2020. The investigation could have stopped there if we treated ExpertData as just another new actor of RaidForums. However, our manual analysis suggested a link between this account and ShinyHunters based on their conversational tactics and databases posted for sale.</p>
<p>The suggested NLP algorithm seems to provide a relevant metric to find connected accounts although with some false positives. Combined manual and automatic analysis showed a potential link of ShinyHunters with the following accounts: fs0c131y (aka whysodankk, whysodank), J4ckd0x, Megadimarus, Databases (aka Eutropius, 012, 0 12), Troy Hunt (aka Jnx3cx). Moreover, accounts belonging to the same user can be automatically detected, like in cases of fs0c131y, Databases, and Troy Hunt. It is also noteworthy that accounts fs0c131y, J4ckd0x and Jnx3cx have similar format of the name.</p>
<div id="attachment_25475-2" style="width: 1690px" class="wp-caption alignnone"><picture><source data-lazy-srcset="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/59-min.jpg.webp" type="image/webp"><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25475-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25475 webpexpress-processed" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg%20xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'%20viewBox='0%200%201680%20900'%3E%3C/svg%3E" alt="The links between ShinyHunters and other accounts in the underground" width="1680" height="900" data-lazy-src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/59-min.jpg"><noscript><img aria-describedby="caption-attachment-25475-2" decoding="async" class="size-full wp-image-25475 webpexpress-processed" src="https://www.group-ib.com/wp-content/uploads/59-min.jpg" alt="The links between ShinyHunters and other accounts in the underground" width="1680" height="900"></noscript></picture><p id="caption-attachment-25475-2" class="wp-caption-text">Figure 58 &#8211; The links between ShinyHunters and other accounts in the underground</p></div>
<p>This article is not about the <strong>ShinyHunters</strong> activity, the group&#8217;s active behavior in the dark web is just a good example for demonstrating automatic techniques that can be used for analyzing discussions. In this case, <strong>we deliberately employ a well-known approach for searching semantically close messages to demonstrate the advantages of even common ML methods in cybersecurity investigations.</strong></p>
<p>As you can see, <strong>the results of the manual method and the method based on the semantics of the messages both overlap and complement each other.</strong> The examination of behavior patterns of accounts in the <a href="https://www.group-ib.com/resources/knowledge-hub/dark-web/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">dark web</a> can show the correlation between accounts used by the same group, with automatic analysis based on the proposed algorithms allowing to increase the depth of research, while reducing the list of suspects. In the article we&#8217;ve examined the following connections:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>between ShinyHunters and fs0c131y</strong> (aka whysodankk, whysodank), whose link with ShinyHunters is quite obvious due to the common contact details;</li>
<li><strong>between ShinyHunters and J4ckd0x, Megadimarus, Databases, Troy Hunt</strong> (aka Jnx3cx) where the link was uncovered using semantic analysis;</li>
<li><strong>between ShinyHunters and ExpertData</strong>, whose link with ShinyHunters was not obvious, and could&#8217;t be revealed by the semantic analysis alone and required some manual work.</li>
</ul>
<p>False positives were related to standard phrases added by the forum administrators to the messages of other members. Moreover, admins reposted the messages of other members which are semantically close to what is searched. Depending on the goal of the analysis these results can be improved by applying more specific filters.</p>
<p>The techniques proposed can be used to speed up and widen the results of the cybercrime investigations related to dark web activities. Once automated, it can even help in the deanonymization process.</p>
</div>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    const postcontent = document.getElementById('blog-post-content');
    const h2counter = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2');

    for (i = 0; i < h2counter.length; i++) {
        const h2elementtag = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i];
        const h2element = postcontent.getElementsByTagName('h2')[i].innerHTML;
        let postnavitem = document.createElement('a');
        postnavitem.classList.add('post-navigation__item', 'subtitle2', 'scrollto');
        postnavitem.innerHTML = h2element;
        const postnavlink = postnavitem.innerHTML;
        let str = postnavlink;
        str = str.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s\u0400-\u04FF]/g, '');
        str = str.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toLowerCase();
        str = str.replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '');
        postnavitem.classList += ' ' + str;
        postnavitem.href = '#' + str;
        h2elementtag.id = str;
        document.getElementById('post-navigation__wrapper').appendChild(postnavitem);
    }

    const sections = document.querySelectorAll('h2');
    const navItems = document.querySelectorAll('.post-navigation__item');

    window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        let current = '';
        sections.forEach((section) => {
            const sectionTop = section.offsetTop;
            const sectionHeight = section.clientHeight;
            if (pageYOffset >= sectionTop - sectionHeight / 10) {
                current = section.getAttribute('id');
            }
        });

        navItems.forEach((a) => {
            a.classList.remove('active');
            if (a.classList.contains(current)) {
                a.classList.add('active');
            }
        });
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrollto').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300,
        );

        return false;
    });

    // SMOOTH SCROLL WITH NO HASH
    var $root = jQuery('html, body');


    jQuery('a[href^="#"].scrolltonohash').click(function() {
        var href = jQuery.attr(this, 'href');
        var headerHeight = 100;

        $root.animate({
                scrollTop: jQuery(href).offset().top - headerHeight
            },
            300
        );

        return false;
    });
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    function readingTime() {
        const text = document.getElementById("blog-post-content").innerText;
        const wpm = 225;
        const words = text.trim().split(/\s+/).length;
        const time = Math.ceil(words / wpm);
        document.getElementById("reading-time").innerText = time;
    }
    readingTime();
</script>

<script type="text/rocketlazyloadscript">
    document.getElementById("share-url").value = window.location.href;
    document.getElementById("share-url-mobile").value = window.location.href;
    jQuery('.share-url__copy').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-url__copy').addClass('success');
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
        setTimeout(() => {
            jQuery('.share-url__copy').removeClass('success');
        }, 1500);
    });

    var carousel123 = new Swiper('.carousel123', {
        grabCursor: true,

            el: '.swiper-pagination',
            clickable: true,
        },
    });

        grabCursor: true,
        pagination: {
            el: '.swiper-pagination',
            clickable: true,
        },
        breakpoints: {

    function positioningSidebar() {
        let sidebar = document.getElementById('sidebar');
        let sidebarHeight = sidebar.offsetHeight;
        let windowHeight = document.documentElement.clientHeight;
        if (windowHeight < (sidebarHeight + 48)) {
            let sidebarAuthors = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__authors');
            let sidebarMorePosts = sidebar.querySelector('.sidebar__more-posts');
            let authorsHeight = sidebarAuthors ? sidebarAuthors.offsetHeight : 0;
            let morePostsHeight = sidebarMorePosts ? sidebarMorePosts.offsetHeight : 0;
            let sidebarTop = authorsHeight + morePostsHeight + 140;
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', -sidebarTop + 'px');
        } else {
            jQuery(sidebar).css('top', '72px');
        }
    }
    window.addEventListener('resize', positioningSidebar);
    positioningSidebar();

    if (localStorage.getItem('theme')) {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
        } else if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'light') {
            jQuery('body').addClass('theme--white');
        }
    } else {
        localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        jQuery('body').addClass('theme--dark');
    }

    jQuery('.theme-toggle').click(function() {
        if (localStorage.getItem('theme') == 'dark') {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'light');
        } else {
            localStorage.setItem('theme', 'dark');
        }
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--dark');
        jQuery('body').toggleClass('theme--white');
        setTimeout(() => {
            jQuery('body').toggleClass('no-transition');
        }, 250);
    });
    jQuery('.share-toggle').click(function() {
        jQuery('.share-toggle').toggleClass('active');
        jQuery('.share-widget').toggleClass('show');
    });
</script>
